# llms.txt — Zscaler Help Portal
# Purpose: Guidance for LLMs and automated agents consuming help.zscaler.com
# Last-Updated: 2026-09-26T17:22:12Z

[site]
name = Zscaler Help Portal
base_url = https://help.zscaler.com
description = Public technical documentation and knowledge content for Zscaler products to help customers learn, configure, troubleshoot, and answer product questions.
publisher = Zscaler, Inc.

[intended_use]
allowed = Summarization, question-answering, retrieval for user support, semantic indexing, and citation-based assistance using publicly available pages on help.zscaler.com.
not_allowed = Training proprietary models on copies of the corpus without permission; bulk republication of full articles; collecting personal data; generating content that impersonates Zscaler Support or represents itself as official case guidance.

[content_scope]
includes = All publicly accessible documentation, guides, troubleshooting articles, and release/feature content hosted on help.zscaler.com.
excludes = Customer-specific data (none is expected on this site); support tickets/cases; any content not hosted on help.zscaler.com.

[access_and_policy]
public_content = true
follow_robots_txt = true
rate_limit = Use conservative request rates, avoid aggressive parallelization, and cache results when possible.
attribution = When answering users, attribute to "Zscaler Help Portal" and include the article title; quote only small excerpts necessary to answer.

[preferred_retrieval]
prefer_canonical_pages = true
avoid = Duplicate URLs created by tracking parameters, faceted navigation, and internal search result pages.

[sitemaps]
sitemap = https://help.zscaler.com/sitemap.xml

[crawling]
allow_paths = /
disallow_paths = /search

[data_handling]
privacy = Do not collect, store, or infer personal data from site content.
security = Do not probe for vulnerabilities or attempt to access non-public infrastructure.

[contact]
support = https://help.zscaler.com/contact-support
legal = https://www.zscaler.com/legal

### [Access Denied Due to Domain Fronting](https://help.zscaler.com/troubleshooting-runbooks/access-denied-due-domain-fronting):In a domain fronting attack, the domain used in the Server Name Indication (SNI) extension or in the Connect request is different from the one supplied later in the Host header. In other words, an attacker hides an HTTP request for a bad website inside a TLS connection to a good website. 

### [Allow Due to Insufficient App Data](https://help.zscaler.com/troubleshooting-runbooks/allow-due-insufficient-app-data):This action is logged by the Service Edge when its Deep Packet Inspection (DPI) engine could not identify the network application. This typically happens when the connection gets terminated before enough packets can be processed to extract the required data.

### [How to Analyze HTTP Headers](https://help.zscaler.com/troubleshooting-runbooks/how-analyze-http-headers):Although most browsers allow exporting HTTP headers with or without data to a .har file, Fiddler typically saves them to a .saz file.

### [Blocked Due to Server Probe Failure](https://help.zscaler.com/troubleshooting-runbooks/blocked-due-server-probe-failure):This runbook article describes if a policy action is triggered and the Zscaler Service Edge cannot make a successful TCP or TLS connection to the target web server. This happens when a web server either does not tolerate SSL Inspection or doesn't complete the TCP handshake with the ZZscaler Service Edge.
